2026-10-11 14:43 | Height engine (C16/C17) | C25 | cause found: NOT the selector (fails with it switched off) nor the level (old 01:40 level fails too). The engine's 2x2 region window asks for the row above the map in the top region row; LA has a ring of empty regions (265/324 entries 0xFFFF), Chicago fills all 342 of the 350-entry table, so above-map indices >= 350 (column 8 on) read past the table → garbage region offset → CD read into address 0 → kernel zeroed. Fix in test: CHI_WORLD=480x630 (16x21 regions = 336 with an empty ring; worldscale.py env switch), variant work/var_world.lev building | chicago/worldscale.py, spots.json (kernelbad)
